# PROGRAM LEG B: SLEEVE METHODS RECHECK, 2026-11-15

**The md2 limitation, printed first as ordered.** The md2 population of 641 sleeves is graded at family granularity only, and only from GrokBot's execute-all record as carried in this workspace (the t474u intake register of 2026-11-14 and Trial Register rows 22-28 and 33). The population's source of authority, cos2-estate `research/x_t473u/t474u_execute_all.md`, is not present in this workspace, and the 641 count itself is carried, not reconciled on this side (master list discrepancy D2). Seven families are named with their frozen constants and source-record OOS notes, covering the 20 named t474u sleeves; the remaining 621 sleeves have no family decomposition available on this side, and they are graded as one residual row whose blocker is named, never smoothed into the named families. Everything about md2 below inherits this limit.

SCOPE: Surface = all 339 desk sleeves in muse-desk-v3 (roster and board at sleeve-activation-calibration-20261111/execution_board.csv) plus the md2 641 at family granularity. Constraints = desk tree read-only throughout; source classes from the 2026-11-12 rule-logic ledger where it covers a sleeve (it covers all 339), else the sleeve's own definition; tiers are the reexamination's five, computed from records only, with Decisions API seats as lens and generation, never selection. Outcome = one itemized row per sleeve with its variables and constants, its firing and outcome record as printed, its mechanism or its absence, its variation answers or queue, and its tier; md2 graded per family. Proof = the per-sleeve register file beside this report, whose row counts reconcile to the coverage table in Section 1, and the seat record with full probability vectors.

## 1. Coverage

| Population | Rows in register | Rows in this report | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| Desk sleeves | 339 | 339 | one row per sleeve, Section 5 |
| md2 families | 8 | 8 | 7 named families (20 named sleeves) + 1 residual row (621), Section 6 |
| Sleeves with a dispatcher entry record | 42 | 42 | 6,708 entry fills, reconciles exactly to Campaign 1's coverage census |
| Sleeves with exit fills only | 23 | 23 | closes against the netted book, no own entries; graded UNGRADABLE-BLOCKED with the exit-only fact in each row's own blocker |
| Sleeves with no dispatcher record at all | 274 | 274 | each graded individually in Section 5 |
| Rule instances attributed per sleeve | 1,946 | (register) | ledger prints 1,832 under its mixed counting convention; reconciliation in Section 3 |
| Seat coverage | 79 items | 79 | 29 desk units + 8 md2 rows at family level; 42 recorded sleeves individually; 802 of 802 questions answered |

Tier census (339): LIVE CANDIDATE 2, WATCH 40, MACHINERY 0, RESOLVED-AGAINST 0, UNGRADABLE-BLOCKED 297.

RESOLVED-AGAINST is zero, and the zero is a finding, not an omission: the estate's own bars for that tier (negative expectancy on at least 3 own-lot closes, or a registered falsifier already spoken) are met by no sleeve. Exactly one sleeve holds negative expectancy at all (bta_onebar_qqq, -$150.25 on 2 closes), one close short of the bar its own program set; it stands at WATCH with the watch named. MACHINERY is zero because every one of the 339 is a strategy object; the producer sleeves (tensor_swing, tensor_red_dip) also trade their own record and are tiered on it, with the producer role stated in their rows.

## 2. Method

- Variables and constants: each sleeve's config entry (module, cadence, session, slice capital, buying-power buffer, flat-by-close, params, inputs, parked) plus every rule instance the 2026-11-12 rule-logic ledger attributes to it, with the instance's verdict as its source class (SOURCED, TESTED, ARBITRARY, RANDOM, ILLOGICAL, DEAD). The register carries each instance's full rule text and file:line pointer; Section 5 prints the verdict counts and the numeric constants extracted from the instance texts.
- Record: the dispatcher record (state/dispatcher.jsonl*, five generations) read with Campaign 1's own event definition (fill events, buy/sell_to_open as entries, buy/sell_to_close as exits). A sleeve's record is its entry stream; exit-only sleeves are named as such. Outcome fields are as printed in the estate's own censuses: frequency.csv populations, ranking.csv Lens P, and the seeded Lens T own-lot closes (lens_t_seeded_rows.json, which diffed zero against the stock build over all 339).
- Variation: for recorded sleeves, three single-variable variations are answered from the record itself: hold horizon (the sleeve's recorded entries priced forward 1, 3, 5 and 10 sessions on stored daily bars, direction-aware, excess over QQQ with the raw return beside it; QQQ is the benchmark of record because the SPY/IWM/DIA caches end 2026-10-31, the campaign's recorded substitution); direction inversion (the negation of the same profile, read off the same numbers); and time-of-day (entries before vs at/after 12:00 ET at the 5-session horizon). Sibling-arm contrasts answer the constant variation where a designed arm sibling also has a record. A variation is called scored only at Campaign 1's pre-registered bar (at least 10 priceable events on at least 3 distinct entry dates); below it, the numbers print as indicative with their counts. For unrecorded sleeves, the row names its variation queue instead.
- One label correction, printed rather than smoothed: the Free Kraken unit was seated under its older label (which carried the word parked from the family's earlier state); the 2026-11-14 CLEAR-THE-BOARD order unparked the family, and the current config carries parked=false for all 339 sleeves, so the unit is named Free Kraken family here. Its seat key (F14) is unchanged.
- Seats: bank `program-b-sleeves-recheck` v1.0 on gpt-6-luna through the governed harness. Seven-lens panels at full width on every seated item (29 desk units, 8 md2 rows, 42 recorded sleeves), an adversarial null per item, an explanation choice per item (repeatable edge / noise / consensus priced / measurement blocked, full vector recorded), an information-value score per family item and per recorded sleeve, and a six-cluster connection bank. Seat answers never set a tier; where a seat and the record part ways, both print.

## 3. Rule-logic ledger reconciliation

Parsed from the five cluster fragments of the 2026-11-12 audit (the ledger's own per-cluster sources) plus the soxl_pm_breakout instance, which lives in the fires fragment's SUMMARY prose rather than its table. Coverage: 339 of 339 sleeves carry at least one attributed instance; family sleeve counts reproduce the ledger's Section 2 exactly (Tensor 30, Aster 127, Lazarus/Q07/MN 85, Spec-built 37, Fires and singles 60). Rows parsed: 515. Sleeve-attributed instances: 1,946, against the ledger's printed 1,832. The difference is the ledger's own mixed convention, printed here rather than smoothed: Aster (615) and Lazarus/Q07/MN (682) reproduce exactly; Tensor parses 308 against 307 printed; the Spec-built fragment counts its 123 grouped ROWS in the ledger's table where this pass attributes each grouped row to every sleeve it names (224 sleeve-instances over the same 123 rows), and the Fires fragment's grouped ENH row (25 instances over the sleeves named) expands the same way (117 sleeve-instances against 105 printed). Per-sleeve source classes in the register follow the per-sleeve attribution throughout.

## 4. Variation answers for the 42 recorded sleeves

At Campaign 1's pre-registered scoring bar (at least 10 priceable events on at least 3 distinct entry dates), 2 of the 42 recorded sleeves are scoreable: aster_etf_qqq_monthly_c098 and aster_etf_qqq_monthly_m150, the same two parents Campaign 1 scored. Both are benchmark-self sleeves (their entries are QQQ itself), so their excess-over-QQQ profile is degenerate by construction and the raw profile is the read: c098 raw forward means +21.3 bp (1 session, n=46), +199.7 bp (5 sessions, n=22); m150's profile is in its register row. The arm contrast between them is the gross-arm constant (0.98 vs 1.5) read on identical signal dates: the records answer the direction of the arm difference only at indicative strength, because m150's priceable count (13) sits near the bar and the two arms share the underlying's path.

Sibling-arm contrasts where both arms hold records (entries / dates / priceable fwd1 / fwd5 raw mean bp):

Hold-horizon and time-of-day reads for every recorded sleeve are in its Section 5 row and in full in the register. The general shape, counted not narrated: priceable fwd1 events total 434 of 6,708 entries (the stored bar cache covers 49 symbols), so most recorded sleeves' variation reads are indicative at small n; the counts print beside every number, and no sleeve's tier rests on an indicative read.

## 5. The 339 sleeves, itemized

Row format: board state and tier; config variables; ledger verdict counts with the constants extracted from its rule instances (full instance texts and file:line pointers in the register); mechanism (the sleeve's own trigger or thesis as printed in its sources, or MECHANISM-ABSENT); record as printed; then the variation answers (recorded sleeves) or the named blocker and variation queue (unrecorded sleeves). Seat lines appear for recorded sleeves (own panel) and every row names its family seat key.

## 8. Spend and utilization

Decisions API, bank `program-b-sleeves-recheck` v1.0, model gpt-6-luna via the governed harness: 5 requests, 802 questions, 802 answers, 183,752 input tokens, $0.0188 at the published $0.10 per 1M uncached input rate (output tokens are not charged). Utilization choices, per the owner's maximum-utilization order: packs built to the endpoint's practical width with shared evidence prefixes (190, 180, 189, 189 and 54 questions per request, under the 400 cap because the seated population is 79 items, not because volume was rationed); all three question types used where each fits (predicate for lens and null seats, choice for explanation and connection structure, score for information value, with full probability vectors recorded for every answer); seven-lens panels at full width on every seated item (79 items x 7 lenses), adversarial nulls and the connection bank as separate banks in the same run. No 429 was hit; the harness's internal backoff never had to fire, and the run stayed far under the 5,000 requests/day circuit breaker (the day ledger, shared with the program's other legs, read 102 requests after this run). The response confidence field was never read, per the typed layer's audit rules.

## 9. Status

STATUS: PARTIAL. Proven on the final artifacts: all 339 desk sleeves itemized in Section 5 and in the register (row counts reconcile to the board's 339; family counts reproduce the rule-logic ledger's exactly; the recorded population reconciles to Campaign 1's census at 42 sleeves and 6,708 entry fills); the md2 641 graded at family granularity in 8 rows with its limitation printed first; 802 of 802 seat questions answered with full vectors. Named open remainder: (1) variation for the 297 unrecorded sleeves is a queue, not an answer, because the dispatcher record does not exist for them, and the queue's first instrument (forward accrual on cured intent records) starts with Monday's session; (2) per-trade outcome grading for 40 of the 42 recorded sleeves waits on separated closes under the virtual book, the same measurement defect FREQUENCY.md named on 2026-11-12 and FIRING-BUT-LOSING confirmed on 2026-11-14; (3) the md2 residual 621 cannot be decomposed until GrokBot's family cards land; (4) seat readings are lens only and carry no tier weight, by the typed layer's standing discipline.
